# Raw VLM Interactions (Multi-GPU Parallel)
# Format: Sample_Index | PROMPT | RESPONSE

Sample 0:
PROMPT:
You are analyzing an image to create a scene graph. A scene graph describes the objects in the scene and the relationships between them.

Please identify:
1. All objects/entities visible in the image
2. The relationships between these objects

Respond in the following format:

OBJECTS:
[object1]
[object2] 
[object3]
...

RELATIONSHIPS:
[object1] [relationship] [object2]
[object2] [relationship] [object3]
...

END

OBJECTS:

table
book
chair

RELATIONSHIPS:

person holding book
book on table
chair near table

END
